# Coverage ledger (generated; agent-read)

Generated by `fvdep-ledger --write` from `FvdepLedger.Rows.ledger` (contract `docs/contracts/U2-core.md` L1–L8); gate stage `coverage` regenerates it + compares the bytes. Edit the rows, then run `bash tools/ledger-doc.sh`.

Root: `main` = `FvdepIO.execute` (interpreter) applied to `FvdepCore.Cli.plan` (shape checked).
Root: `FvdepIO.Step.main` = `FvdepIO.Step.execute` (interpreter) applied to `FvdepIO.Step.plan` (shape checked).
Root: `FvdepIO.Parse.main` = `FvdepIO.Parse.execute` (interpreter) applied to `FvdepIO.Parse.plan` (shape checked).
Step: `FvdepCore.Oracle.Driver.driverText` writes a sandbox step's program from the texts of `FvdepCore.Protocol.Types`, `FvdepCore.Oracle.Wire` (`FvdepCore.Oracle.Driver.embedded`; their import lines dropped), calling `FvdepCore.Oracle.Wire.answer` (`FvdepCore.Oracle.Driver.call`).

## `FvdepCore.Cli`

- Contract: U1 K2; docs/interface.md §1–§2; U2 X5
- Claim: Outside `fvdep index [--check]` (row `FvdepCore.Index.Command`) + a well-formed `fvdep oracle` (row `FvdepCore.Oracle.Command`, run with SHA-256 + T's default limits), every invocation writes one stream alone: exit 0 with stderr empty, or exit 2 with its usage on stderr (`usageOf`: `oracle`'s own for an `oracle` line) + stdout empty. The plan performs exactly the outcome's outputs (stdout, then stderr), an interrupted run a prefix of them; it never reads or replaces a file.
- Subject: `FvdepCore.Cli.plan`
- Path: `main` → `FvdepCore.Cli.plan`
- Statement `FvdepLedger.Rows.cliStreams` (hash 0x927654a6), proved by `FvdepCore.Cli.run_streams`:

  ```lean
  ∀ (args : List String),
    (FvdepCore.Cli.run args).exit = 0 ∧ (FvdepCore.Cli.run args).stderr = "" ∨
      (FvdepCore.Cli.run args).exit = 2 ∧
        (FvdepCore.Cli.run args).stdout = "" ∧
          (FvdepCore.Cli.run args).stderr = FvdepCore.Cli.usageOf args
  ```
- Statement `FvdepLedger.Rows.cliPlanComplete` (hash 0x6c3c8199), proved by `FvdepCore.Cli.plan_complete`:

  ```lean
  ∀ (args : List String),
    ¬FvdepCore.Cli.PlanArgs args →
      ∀ (evs : List (FvdepCore.Eff × FvdepCore.Response)) (code : UInt32),
        (FvdepCore.Cli.plan args).Runs evs (some code) ↔
          evs = FvdepCore.Cli.outputs (FvdepCore.Cli.run args) ∧ code = (FvdepCore.Cli.run args).exit
  ```
- Statement `FvdepLedger.Rows.cliPlanInterrupted` (hash 0x3ac3b0fb), proved by `FvdepCore.Cli.plan_interrupted`:

  ```lean
  ∀ (args : List String),
    ¬FvdepCore.Cli.PlanArgs args →
      ∀ (evs : List (FvdepCore.Eff × FvdepCore.Response)),
        (FvdepCore.Cli.plan args).Runs evs none →
          evs <+: FvdepCore.Cli.outputs (FvdepCore.Cli.run args)
  ```
- Uninterpreted: `FvdepIO.Hash.sha256`
- Residue: `interpreter` (the IO interpreter's effect → primitive mapping (`FvdepIO.Run`)), `hacl-sha256` (SHA-256: HACL*'s C (verified upstream in F*/Low*: memory safety + functional correctness vs FIPS 180-4) + fvdep's FFI shim, chunk + streaming orchestration (`c/fvdep_sha256*.c`, `FvdepIO.Hash`)), `runtime` (Lean compiler + runtime (compiled code = the kernel definition; string + IO primitives))
- Unproven: none

## `FvdepCore.Plan`

- Contract: U2 X1
- Claim: The trace semantics of effect plans: a finished plan performs nothing; a step is interrupted or performs its effect with a fitting response; runs of `p >>= f` decompose into runs of `p` and of `f`.
- Subject: `FvdepCore.Plan.bind`
- Path: none yet (wired by U8)
- Statement `FvdepLedger.Rows.planPure` (hash 0xad92f22a), proved by `FvdepCore.Plan.runs_pure_iff`:

  ```lean
  ∀ {α : Type} (a : α) (evs : List (FvdepCore.Eff × FvdepCore.Response)) (r : Option α),
    (FvdepCore.Plan.pure a).Runs evs r ↔ evs = [] ∧ r = some a
  ```
- Statement `FvdepLedger.Rows.planStep` (hash 0x49e402d9), proved by `FvdepCore.Plan.runs_step_iff`:

  ```lean
  ∀ {α : Type} (e : FvdepCore.Eff) (k : FvdepCore.Response → FvdepCore.Plan α)
    (evs : List (FvdepCore.Eff × FvdepCore.Response)) (r : Option α),
    (FvdepCore.Plan.step e k).Runs evs r ↔
      evs = [] ∧ r = none ∨
        ∃ resp rest, evs = (e, resp) :: rest ∧ FvdepCore.Fits e resp ∧ (k resp).Runs rest r
  ```
- Statement `FvdepLedger.Rows.planBindSome` (hash 0x19895492), proved by `FvdepCore.Plan.runs_bind_some`:

  ```lean
  ∀ {α β : Type} (p : FvdepCore.Plan α) (f : α → FvdepCore.Plan β)
    (evs : List (FvdepCore.Eff × FvdepCore.Response)) (b : β),
    (p.bind f).Runs evs (some b) ↔
      ∃ evs₁ evs₂ a, evs = evs₁ ++ evs₂ ∧ p.Runs evs₁ (some a) ∧ (f a).Runs evs₂ (some b)
  ```
- Statement `FvdepLedger.Rows.planBindNone` (hash 0xf6b91c6c), proved by `FvdepCore.Plan.runs_bind_none`:

  ```lean
  ∀ {α β : Type} (p : FvdepCore.Plan α) (f : α → FvdepCore.Plan β)
    (evs : List (FvdepCore.Eff × FvdepCore.Response)),
    (p.bind f).Runs evs none ↔
      p.Runs evs none ∨
        ∃ evs₁ evs₂ a, evs = evs₁ ++ evs₂ ∧ p.Runs evs₁ (some a) ∧ (f a).Runs evs₂ none
  ```

- Residue: none
- Unproven: none

## `FvdepIO.Run`

- Contract: U1 K2 (failed output write → exit 2); U2 X6
- Claim: `main`'s interpreter performs each effect on the real streams; a failed output write exits 2, with `Could not write the output: <reason>.` on stderr unless the reader closed the pipe.

## `FvdepCore.Evidence`

- Contract: U2 E1–E7; Decisions Conformance method + Verification profile; docs/interface.md §4 + §6
- Claim: A result depends only on the attributed observations (the request's challenge key + export bindings; identical duplicates count once). PROVEN ⇔ a passing proof's observations (implementation profile, both builds, statement, graph, axioms inside the allowlist, all three kernels) with nothing contradicting them. Else the first attributed infrastructure observation (exit 2, negatives kept), else the phase rule: the first failed phase's reasons (NOT PROVEN), a missing phase or a protocol violation → `internal` (negatives kept); reasons appear exactly on NOT PROVEN + MISMATCH. Differential evidence is MATCHED or MISMATCH by its supported comparisons (none → `adapter`), never PROVEN, and proof requests ignore it.
- Subject: `FvdepCore.Evidence.result`

## `FvdepCore.Status`

- Contract: U2 P1–P9 + X2–X4; Decisions Proof policy + Lifecycle + Data formats; docs/interface.md §2–§6
- Claim: Policy acceptance; exit codes per module + command (`check` from its fresh results alone); receipts only for well-shaped requests, the verdict = the evidence kind; staleness + lock changes = exactly the differing fields, in report order; the exact file disagreements; the first status reason with its payload (delivered ⇔ none); retraction = the first matching notice (monotone, never delivered); `status` exit per policy (the files agree first); the lock decision (a refusal exits as `check` does); the commit step: a refusal performs no effect, a commit writes the write set in canonical order + exits 0 or stops at the first failure with its diagnostic + exit 2, and each file ends old or new.
- Subject: `FvdepCore.Status.authorize`
- Path: none yet (wired by U8)


## `FvdepCore.Json.Value`

- Contract: U3 J1 + J5; Decisions Data formats
- Claim: `Value.object` = the members stably sorted by key; `get?` = the first equal key's value (`none` when absent or not an object); each projection = exactly its constructor's payload.
- Subject: `FvdepCore.Json.Value.get?`
- Path: none (a supporting component)
- Statement `FvdepLedger.Rows.JsonClaims.objectLaw` (hash 0x92c070e9), proved by `FvdepAudit.Json.Proof.object_law`:

  ```lean
  ∀ (ms : List (String × FvdepCore.Json.Value)),
    ∃ ms',
      FvdepCore.Json.Value.object ms = FvdepCore.Json.Value.obj ms' ∧
        ms'.Perm ms ∧
          List.Pairwise (fun a b => a.fst ≤ b.fst) ms' ∧
            ∀ (k : String),
              List.filter (fun x => x.fst == k) ms' = List.filter (fun x => x.fst == k) ms
  ```
- Statement `FvdepLedger.Rows.JsonClaims.getLaw` (hash 0xa63db91e), proved by `FvdepAudit.Json.Proof.get_law`:

  ```lean
  (∀ (ms : List (String × FvdepCore.Json.Value)) (k : String) (w : FvdepCore.Json.Value),
      (FvdepCore.Json.Value.obj ms).get? k = some w ↔
        ∃ pre post,
          ms = pre ++ (k, w) :: post ∧ ∀ (p : String × FvdepCore.Json.Value), p ∈ pre → p.fst ≠ k) ∧
    (∀ (ms : List (String × FvdepCore.Json.Value)) (k : String),
        (FvdepCore.Json.Value.obj ms).get? k = none ↔
          ∀ (p : String × FvdepCore.Json.Value), p ∈ ms → p.fst ≠ k) ∧
      ∀ (v : FvdepCore.Json.Value) (k : String),
        (∀ (ms : List (String × FvdepCore.Json.Value)), v ≠ FvdepCore.Json.Value.obj ms) →
  ```

## `FvdepIO.Sys.Policy`

- Contract: U3 F2 + F4
- Claim: `replace`'s calls + result follow F2's protocol written apart (temp walk, all bytes, fsync, one close, one rename step, `syncDir`; cleanup closes once, then unlinks only its own temp; causes in order; `durability`), which fixes the result; one rename step is the only call naming the target; over the directory model the target holds its old state or the new bytes after any run or interruption (new on success), which is U2 X4's `Holds` step for a present target; a run reads its answers in order (call k + whether it is issued depend on answers 0…k−1 alone). - Claim: `replace`, `readFile`, `withWriterLock`, `Writer` + `syncDir` run the proven policy over `syscalls` (each request → its one syscall export, run in the policy's order + answered once; the native writer guard: `Writer.create` registers the created descriptor (`writerNew`), each later `Writer` operation runs between `writerAcquire` + `writerRelease`, a guarded close marks the guard closed (`writerMarkClosed`) before its one close, the guard's finalizer closes a descriptor still open; residue `sys-shim`).
- Subject: `FvdepIO.Sys.replace`
- Path: none (a supporting component)
- Uninterpreted: `FvdepIO.Sys.Native.write`, `FvdepIO.Sys.Native.close`, `FvdepIO.Sys.Native.unlink`, `FvdepIO.Sys.Native.lock`, `FvdepIO.Sys.Native.openFile`, `FvdepIO.Sys.Native.openLock`, `FvdepIO.Sys.Native.writerMarkClosed`, `FvdepIO.Sys.Native.sync`, `FvdepIO.Sys.Native.openDir`, `FvdepIO.Sys.Native.rename`, `FvdepIO.Sys.Native.read`, `FvdepIO.Sys.Native.openRead`
- Residue: `os` (OS, filesystem + process semantics), `runtime` (Lean compiler + runtime (compiled code = the kernel definition; string + IO primitives))
- Unproven: `sys-shim` (the persistence syscall shim `c/fvdep_sys.c` (one libc call per export, errno at once: the open variants, read, write, fsync, close, rename, unlink, flock) + its native writer guard (closed flag, mutex, finalizer close-if-open) + `FvdepIO.Sys`'s instantiation of the proven policy (`syscalls`: each request → its export, run once in the policy's order with its answer returned; the read buffer; the guard's closed mark; `withWriterLock` re-raising the act's exception after the close))

  ```lean
    (cap : Nat),
    FvdepCore.Phase.snapDecide l used leaf o = FvdepCore.Phase.Snap.read cap ↔
      leaf = true ∧
        ∃ size,
          o = FvdepCore.Phase.Opened.file FvdepCore.Phase.Kind.regular size ∧
            size ≤ l.fileBytes ∧
  ```

## `FvdepIO.Profile`

- Contract: U6 P1, P5a
- Claim: The supervisor stages the request + the frozen batch + the parse child beside the step helper, runs the parse step through U5's runner (its deadline = P6's `seconds`) + hands its observation to `judge` as P5c's `Run`; a library's rows = each trusted header's explicit imports in source order.
- Subject: `FvdepIO.Profile.runStep`
- Path: none yet (wired by U8)
- Uninterpreted: `FvdepIO.Spawn.bwrap`
- Residue: `os` (OS, filesystem + process semantics), `runtime` (Lean compiler + runtime (compiled code = the kernel definition; string + IO primitives))
- Unproven: `sandbox-spawn` (the sandbox runner's IO: `c/fvdep_spawn.c`'s supervisor exports (bwrap by `posix_spawn` with four pipes above descriptor 4, every descriptor ≥ 5 closed (`closefrom`, resolved at run time: absent → `ENOSYS`, never a spawn), an empty environment, every signal at its default + unmasked; poll, kill, waitpid) + `FvdepIO.Sandbox` (the pumps, the deadline's SIGKILL + a 5 s drain bound, the observation handed to the proven `classify` + `read`)), `lean-parser` (the profile's parse: `fvdep-parse` (`FvdepIO.Parse`: the request + sources read at `/fvdep/src`, the digests compared, Lean's `parseHeader` + `parseCommand` under each module's grammar — `importModules (loadExts := true)` over the toolchain's modules at `/fvdep/sysroot` alone, each clean header's toolchain imports first read present at Lean's module path there (I1) —, P5's failures read from the message log, the `Syntax` → tree conversion under P6's caps + C1's cap location, the record written) + the supervisor's in-process header reads of trusted library modules (`FvdepIO.Profile.headerImports`))

## Trusted residue

- `interpreter` — the IO interpreter's effect → primitive mapping (`FvdepIO.Run`): an IO primitive has no Lean semantics to prove against; the U1 K2 smoke cases test the output mapping (full device, broken pipe) (ruling: "the IO interpreter's effect → primitive mapping (tested)")
- `profile-table` — the implementation profile's v1 table (`FvdepCore.Profile.Table`: K's kinds + their atom sets + slot rules, S, the generated declaration names, the deriving classes): data has no behaviour to prove; contract U6's layer 2: reviewed against researcher-16's lists + each kind's parser tokens (every leftover token classed), pinned by R's planted modules (`lake test` ProfileC; tester-8's suite from U6-C) + U6-D's freeze probe (ruling: "the v1 table = reviewed kernel data (residue `profile-table`)")
- `lean-parser` — the profile's parse: `fvdep-parse` (`FvdepIO.Parse`: the request + sources read at `/fvdep/src`, the digests compared, Lean's `parseHeader` + `parseCommand` under each module's grammar — `importModules (loadExts := true)` over the toolchain's modules at `/fvdep/sysroot` alone, each clean header's toolchain imports first read present at Lean's module path there (I1) —, P5's failures read from the message log, the `Syntax` → tree conversion under P6's caps + C1's cap location, the record written) + the supervisor's in-process header reads of trusted library modules (`FvdepIO.Profile.headerImports`): Lean's parser + the `Syntax` projection have no fvdep-owned semantics to prove against (contract U6 N): that the checked tree = the tree the build elaborates is the residue; the walk + the batch's conclusion over the observed trees are claimed (rows `FvdepCore.Profile.*`); tested by tester-8's blind suite (stage `profile-suite`: R's planted modules, the caps at their limits + one over, the step faults, the records) + `lake test` ProfileC (a 20,000-term left-nested chain past `depth`, non-UTF-8 bytes, an import the toolchain lacks, each table form on Lean's own trees) (ruling: "the Lean parser + the `Syntax` projection (residue `lean-parser`)")
- `runtime` — Lean compiler + runtime (compiled code = the kernel definition; string + IO primitives): the toolchain is trusted (Decisions `Self-verification scope`) (ruling: "Lean compiler + runtime")
- `os` — OS, filesystem + process semantics: outside Lean; fvdep's effects reach them through the interpreter (ruling: "OS, filesystem + process semantics")
- `hacl-sha256` — SHA-256: HACL*'s C (verified upstream in F*/Low*: memory safety + functional correctness vs FIPS 180-4) + fvdep's FFI shim, chunk + streaming orchestration (`c/fvdep_sha256*.c`, `FvdepIO.Hash`): C has no Lean semantics: HACL*'s proof = upstream evidence (no collision-resistance claim); the shim + orchestration are small, reviewed + tested: `lake test` (FIPS 180-4 examples incl. 10^6 × `a`, chunked `update` = one-shot, shared-state branching) + S5's blind suite (stage `codec-suite`, 1,212 cases: NIST CAVP short, long + Monte vectors, `sha256sum` differentials, update splits, chunk arithmetic + the total limit at lowered ceilings, `ofReader`, `sha256File`, concurrent Tasks; the C core under gcc ASan + UBSan, zero leaks) (ruling: "HACL* (verified upstream) + FFI wrapper (tested)")
- `toml-parse` — Lake's TOML parser + elaborator (`Lake.Toml.loadToml`): text → table, repeated + redefined keys refused, the first error's position + message: toolchain code (Decisions: trusted); `ofLake` names any key of another shape; `fvdep.toml` ≤ 64 KiB bounds its recursion depth (T1); tested by `lake test` (a printed manifest reads back, syntax + repeated keys refuse with Lake's line, a BOM refuses first) + T6's blind suite (stage `manifest-suite`: 1,850 credited cases through Lake's parser, `ofLake` + `validate`; the 4 `T.worst.*` cap cases = uncredited evidence of the parser at the cap: no fvdep mutant reaches them + a driver stack down to 256 KiB leaves them green) (ruling: "Lake.Toml + Lake comparison code (toolchain)")
- `sys-shim` — the persistence syscall shim `c/fvdep_sys.c` (one libc call per export, errno at once: the open variants, read, write, fsync, close, rename, unlink, flock) + its native writer guard (closed flag, mutex, finalizer close-if-open) + `FvdepIO.Sys`'s instantiation of the proven policy (`syscalls`: each request → its export, run once in the policy's order with its answer returned; the read buffer; the guard's closed mark; `withWriterLock` re-raising the act's exception after the close): C + the OS have no Lean semantics: each export is one reviewed syscall; the policy over them is proven (rows `FvdepIO.Sys.Policy`); the mapping is tested by `lake test` (replace over absent, present, symlink + directory targets, missing directory, read back, absent + empty reads, the caller's read cap, writer lock contention + exception, Writer lifecycle) + F6's blind suite (stage `sys-suite`, 2,819 credited cases through the dev driver `fvdep-dev`: an LD_PRELOAD fault injector scripting each syscall (every op × errno, `EINTR` budgets, short + zero reads and writes), a crash cut at every syscall of a `replace`, lock contention between processes, ZFS + the gate host's filesystem, the shim + guard under gcc ASan + UBSan) + fuzz target `sys-actions` (action sequences over the shim + guard; corpus replayed under gcc ASan + UBSan by stage `fuzz-replay`) (ruling: "the persistence syscall shim + writer guard (tested)")
- `lean-env` — `fvdep index`'s observation filler (`FvdepIO.Index`): the presence probe + `lean-toolchain`'s bytes; the snapshot (the three root modules + the `.lean` files of `Fvdep`, `FvdepPreview` + `FvdepProofs` copied into a per-process run directory `.lake/fvdep-index/<pid>/`, removed at the end; a link followed for its content + marked irregular, a special file never opened); the build (Lake from fvdep's toolchain in a clean environment: PATH, HOME, ELAN_HOME, Lake's artifact cache off) + its first `error:` line, Lake's progress relayed through the line rule; the load (`importModules` over the built oleans) + the facts read from that environment (declarations, marks, module imports + files, entry files, ABI application types, spec-type comparisons), `collectAxioms` per `FvdepProofs` theorem, the registry record fields, the kernel's verdict on each certificate literal (`Kernel.check` + `isDefEq`, synchronous); `leanchecker` over the built modules + its first rejection; `index.json`'s read + replace (`FvdepIO.Sys`): the toolchain's environment + Lake and `leanchecker` as processes have no Lean semantics to prove against: the observations are the plan claims' premise (Decisions U4 C-rules: `Facts`); tested by tester-5's blind suite (stage `index-suite`, 701 cases through the built `fvdep`: each C9 refusal class with its message, the schema-1 bytes + the entry count, identity (digest inputs: comments, proofs + unrelated entries), purity (planted artifacts, environment + an absolute path leave the bytes unchanged; `--check` + drift leave `index.json` untouched), two concurrent runs, load + write faults injected by an `LD_PRELOAD` library, Lake's progress through the line rule) + `tests/cli/smoke.sh` (`index --help`, `help index`, usage) (ruling: "`fvdep index`'s observation filler (tested)")
- `literal` — literal data (the version string `Fvdep.version`): a constant has no behaviour to prove; the gate's `meta` stage holds it equal to both lakefiles (ruling: "Literal data (the version string) is checked by the gate's `meta` stage")
- `bwrap` — bubblewrap (≥ 0.8.0): how it reads the arguments fvdep emits (`bwrapRead`: each option by its name + arity, `--` ends the options) + what each operation does (namespaces, read-only binds, sized tmpfs, read-only remounts, the status lines on descriptor 3): an OS tool with no Lean semantics; the claims hold the arguments fvdep builds against a model of bwrap's reading, written from its manual (bubblewrap 0.11.1); the CI smoke + U5-B's isolation tests (G1) check the real tool on vflab + the CI runner (ruling: "bwrap")
- `lean-toolchain` — `lean` + `leanexport` as processes: a compile's exit code (Lean refusing its input exits 1), its outputs + diagnostics, the export's bytes: the toolchain is trusted (Decisions `Self-verification scope`: Lean parser, elaborator + exporter); P5's verdicts map its exit codes, tested by tester-6's blind suite through `fvdep-dev` (U5-D) (ruling: "Lean parser, elaborator + exporter")
- `cache-fs` — the build cache's filesystem effects: publication (temp, fsync, rename, directory fsync), per-key locks + pins, the observed entry listings + use stamps eviction + validation decide over: filesystem semantics have no Lean model here; the pure policies decide over observed state (K5, K6), the effects are tested by K7's work counts + tester-6's cache cases (U5-C) (ruling: "the build cache's filesystem effects")
- `kernels` — the three kernels as processes — leanchecker, con-leche `--verified`, NanoDa in strict axiom mode: each one's verdict on e2 + the bytes it prints: external checkers fvdep runs, trusted by diversity (Decisions `Verification profile`: Lean's default kernel, con-leche's machine-checked consistency theorem for `--verified`, NanoDa an independent implementation); K2's table maps each exit code + output (texts from the 4.35 sources, each class measured on the pinned binaries: C's raw controls, stage `verification-canaries`, + `R.forged`); C's canaries run each against known-good + known-false inputs (ruling: "kernels (diversity above)")
- `toolchain-install` — the toolchain install the kernel steps bind read-only: the files T1 fingerprinted stay the bytes of its last full hash (T1 reuses its cache record while the tree's stat stamp holds: the stamp detects a change, it is no content identity) + the three binaries K4 hashes in this command stay those bytes through each step's bind: the user's own files + fvdep's own cache record (contract U7 K4: a modification of one's own toolchain or cache that keeps T1's stat stamp is outside the threat model); T1's stamp + K4's per-command rehash of the three binaries (ruling: "premise `toolchain-install`")
- `kernel-steps` — `FvdepIO.Judge`'s IO: the three kernel binaries hashed, e2 + NanoDa's config + D3's stubs staged into a fresh directory (the copy re-hashed), each kernel step through `FvdepIO.Sandbox.run`: filesystem + process effects without a Lean model; the pin decision (`pinCheck`) + the status table (`kernelStatus`) are pure + claimed; the IO is tested by `lake test`'s `judge D3` stub kernels through the real runner + `judge K4` on the installed toolchain (vflab + CI) (ruling: "the IO interpreter's effect → primitive mapping (tested)")
- `sandbox-spawn` — the sandbox runner's IO: `c/fvdep_spawn.c`'s supervisor exports (bwrap by `posix_spawn` with four pipes above descriptor 4, every descriptor ≥ 5 closed (`closefrom`, resolved at run time: absent → `ENOSYS`, never a spawn), an empty environment, every signal at its default + unmasked; poll, kill, waitpid) + `FvdepIO.Sandbox` (the pumps, the deadline's SIGKILL + a 5 s drain bound, the observation handed to the proven `classify` + `read`): C + the OS have no Lean semantics: each export is one reviewed call; the outcome + frame reading over the runner's observations are proven (rows `FvdepCore.Sandbox`, `FvdepCore.Step`); the IO is tested by tester-6's blind D2 cases (stage `sandbox-suite`) + the isolation cases (stage `isolation`, each killed by a mutant of `tests/isolation/mutants.py`) (ruling: "the sandbox spawn shim")
- `step-helper` — `fvdep-step`, PID 2 of every step: its arguments, non-dumpable, descriptor 4 taken only as an inherited pipe, `RLIMIT_CORE` = 1 + `RLIMIT_FSIZE`, the output directories, its child (`posix_spawnp`), the kill-all (refused in C unless PID 2), the `statvfs` samples, the regular files under `/fvdep/work/out` read without following a link, the frame written on descriptor 4: C + the OS have no Lean semantics: the frame it writes reads back exactly (row `FvdepCore.Step`, `frameRead`); its IO is tested by the isolation cases (stage `isolation`: G1.helper, G1.grandchild, G1.core, G4.file-cap, R8.channel, G1.killall-guard, each killed by a mutant) + tester-6's D2 frame cases (ruling: "the step helper's IO")
- `project-snapshot` — the project snapshot's reads: `c/fvdep_snapshot.c`'s `openat` of one plain path component (`O_NOFOLLOW`, `O_NONBLOCK`, `O_NOCTTY`, `O_CLOEXEC`; any other name → `EINVAL`) + `fstat` on its descriptor, the project directory's open, the reads under their caps, every descriptor closed on every path (`FvdepIO.Snapshot.walk`): C + the OS have no Lean semantics: the export is one reviewed `openat` + `fstat` pair; each step's decision is pure (`snapNames`, `snapDecide`, `snapRead`); the IO is tested by `lake test`'s snapshot cases (a link at the file or on the way, a FIFO without a writer, a socket, a directory as the file, a file on the way, missing paths, names outside T3's grammar, both caps at n and n + 1, the shim's name guard, descriptors on every path) (ruling: "the project snapshot's reads")
- `failure-bundle` — B2's failure bundles (`FvdepIO.Bundle.write`: the inputs listed + copied, the record, staging + rename under `bundles/lock`, retention's removals) + B1's reason text + B2's picks (`omitOrder`, `retainPick`): presentation + a debugging aid: no verdict reads a bundle or a reason's text (P5 classifies; a `failed` reason's text = B1's presentation, like U3's render ruling); tested by `lake test` (reason, picks) + tester-6's bundle cases (B.retention; B.complete + B.incomplete with D4) (ruling: "the failure bundle's writes")
- `git` — git as a process (U5b L3–L4): `c/fvdep_spawn.c`'s `fvdep_spawn_proc` (`posix_spawnp` through the caller's `PATH`, its own process group, an exact environment, stdin from a file or `/dev/null`, stdout + stderr pipes above descriptor 2, every descriptor ≥ 3 closed) + `fvdep_spawn_killpg` (0, 1, a negative value + the caller's own group refused) + `FvdepIO.Git.run` (the pumps, stdout's cap, stderr's head, the group killed at the deadline or past the cap before the reap, a 5 s drain bound) + what git does with L3's configuration over a remote (fetch + fsck, `cat-file`, `rev-parse`, `ls-tree`): an external program over a remote, with no Lean semantics: its two machine outputs are read by proved readers (`parseTree`, `parseBatch`) + the selection decided by the proved tables; tested by `lake test`'s library cases (real git over local fixture repositories: head, pin, warm pin, offline head, never fetched, received failures; the transport classifier over git 2.53.0's measured lines; a fake git: the exact environment + argv, the deadline's group kill, diagnostics scrubbed + escaped) + tester-7's L cases (U5b-O) (ruling: "`git` (an external program over a remote)")
- `library-store` — the library store's filesystem effects (U5b L2, L5) + P1–P2's reads: the lock (`flock` polled within `lockSeconds`), stale temps removed, staging (files written 0644 + fsynced, directories fsynced, the `.complete` marker last), the rename + `trees/` fsync, `head.json`'s F2 replace + read, links never followed when removing; each `fvdep.toml` candidate by `lstat`, the chosen one read through U3's reader: filesystem semantics have no Lean model here; the selection + every refusal over the observed state are the proved tables (`start`, `afterPin`, `afterHead`) or tested texts; tested by `lake test`'s library cases (layout + modes, incomplete trees rebuilt, damaged records, the lock's timeout, a pin's warm serve) + tester-7's L + P cases (U5b-O) (ruling: "`library-store` (filesystem effects)")
- `oracle-driver` — the evaluation step's driver (U5b W5, V3): `Driver.lean`'s file IO (`requests.txt` read, `/fvdep/work/out/replies.txt` written), the one generated line naming `@S.fn`, type-class resolution choosing W's `Native`/`Answer`/`Serve` instances for the spec's argument + result types (the bodies of `answer` + those instances, the dictionaries supplied: `ServeClaims`), the toolchain elaborating + interpreting the text (`lean --run`) inside a U5 step over the snapshot's view: the step runs the snapshot's code under the toolchain, + fvdep owns no proof of Lean's elaborator or interpreter; the conversions it runs are W's proved functions, shipped as their source (`include_str` + `lake test`'s Q4 byte check); tested by tester-7's W cases (one fixture entry per served representation, a 3-argument implicit-binder one, generated values vs independent references) + tester-1's `oracle.*` cases (ruling: "`oracle-driver` (the toolchain elaborating + interpreting the driver text)")
